Testimony of CRIVA "DE DOUZA MELO
On the 11th day of the month of October, 1920, he attended the meeting of the Board of Directors of the Caioba, residing in the state of Bahia, identified by the number 32029, expediented by the Public Security Secretariat of Rio Grande do Norte, native of Mossoró/RB, in order to give testimony regarding the event related in the TLX E-2C2053Z of the 11th District of Itaval.
He informed that on July 27, 1930, approximately at 1:50 p.m., he was in command of the Caioba and in the estimated position (0 5 ° 13' S - 03 5 ° 09' ) at a distance of 10,000 nautical miles from Bitintinja, navigating on the magnetic course of 100° and traveling from Ubaibldn (Fetro ERAS platform) to Matai. At that moment, he noticed a bright light, with an intense light coming from an object located at an angle of 30° to the right of the course; it reached a point where he thought it might collide; he saw a flash (navigation light). In the following 10 minutes, the object made a (apparent) movement in relation to the ship, as shown in Figure III-1.

As the analysis of the "TRACES" (or "EVIDENCES") seen in the video did not provide reasons to believe a take-off of an interceptor was necessary, the command informed this fact to the control center (COVA) and at 22:00 (or thereabouts), deactivated the alert status. The two combat crews of the 1st Air Defense Group (1° GVA) remained on high alert.

The officials present at the command post (COPM) on the night of 19/FEV/80 left at 22:40, but the service team in the Air Defense room remained alert, noting any modifications to the situation and activating the overwatch officer of the COPL again.

DECLARATION
--- seg-fcfc178f248dd57e26c43881 [needs-review] ---
I, IS Q RT COM LAERTF BASTOS MATHIAS, was serving on 19/19
GTT, and I declare that approximately at 5:35 PM on the date of 26/08/80,
while returning from Santa Maria in aircraft C-115 2350, having
onboard crew members Cel Mafra (? P), Cap J. Lopes (IP), IS Mathias (1RT),
3S Claudio (IMC), in the Oleo/Afonsoi's sector, we sent a visual report of the
aircraft for direct heading, commanded by IP, at an altitude of approximately
1,000 FT. Immediate action was taken to contact ACC RJ regarding the existence
of any known traffic in the area, obtaining a negative response.

Subsequently, it came to my knowledge that the "visual report" was prompted by
the existence of a bright, unidentified object heading towards a collision.

Detailed information was not captured at the time, and I was in the process of
loading cargo in the past.

There is nothing further to declare.

===== PAGE 92 [page-70f02a6c9028f808dffd9c0f] =====
--- seg-aa817245f7b418a88a97c7f0 [machine-unreviewed] ---
DECLARATION
--- seg-2fad624deaf88a8b69cb8cc0 [needs-review] ---
I, 3S Q ANV PEDRO CLÁUDIO DE FARIAS, currently serving in the 19/19 CTT, declare that on the day 26 August 1980, during my return from Santa Maria to FAB 2350 avista at 17:35P, near the location of São João do Meriti, I observed an unidentified object with great intensity of light, resembling a large aircraft, possibly a correction for the flight path of the 09 pist of the Galeão.

I also observed a nearly vertical trajectory of the object, which appeared to be descending and approaching at the time. The lights were constant and were maintaining the same altitude. Immediately after the pilot proceeded, the lights faded, leaving no further visible trace or shadow.

There is nothing further to declare.

===== PAGE 97 [page-79b33fa8ee251b909e283e4a] =====
--- seg-c284afe2fe8e9c423cc94229 [machine-unreviewed] ---

--- seg-81f5079a35238fd975b0b60c [machine-unreviewed] ---
DECLARATION
--- seg-3f933d1318a76cd8f1c87925 [needs-review] ---
I, IS Q RT COM LAERTE BASTOS MATHIAS, was serving on 19/19
GTT, at approximately 5:35 PM on the day August 26, 1980, while returning from Santa Maria in the C-115 2350 air, with passengers Cel Mafra (2P), Capt J. Lopes (IP), IS Mathias (IRD, 3S Claudio (1MC), in the Galeão/Afonsos aircraft, we received a distress call from an aircraft flying directly ahead, commanded by IP, at an altitude of roughly 1,000 feet. Immediate action was taken to contact ACC RJ regarding the existence of any known traffic in the area, receiving a negative response.

Subsequently, it came to my knowledge that the "distress call" was caused by an unidentified, brilliant object that we encountered on a collision course.

Further details are not available to provide, as at that moment I was in the co-pilot's seat, handling navigation equipment on board.

There is nothing further to declare.

--- seg-e5d1343743e3aa3b8e1a4d2e [machine-unreviewed] ---
DECLARATION
--- seg-d73ec47ee1faee41d0ccc7ed [needs-review] ---
I, Pedro Cláudio de Farias, am currently serving in the 19/19 GTT, and I declare that on the 26th of April 1980, while returning to Santa Maria from FAB 2350, at approximately 5:35 PM, near the location of Biojo de Marit, I observed an unidentified object with great intensity of light, which appeared to be a large aircraft, possibly a Galeão (a type of ship).

I also observed a nearly vertical trajectory of the object, suggesting a correction in its final longitute.

At that moment, the pilot proceeded to deviate from his route, and the light went out, leaving no trace or visible evidence.

There is nothing else to declare.
